    80    const num = parseWholeNumber(value);

    82    return num;
    83  }

    93    const parsed = Date.parse(value);

    95    return new Date(parsed).toISOString();

   172  function hashPassword(password, salt) {
   173    return crypto.pbkdf2Sync(password, salt, 120000, 32, 
'sha256').toString('hex');
   174  }
   175  
   176  function verifyPassword(password, user) {
   177    const actual = hashPassword(password, user.password_salt);
   178    const expected = Buffer.from(user.password_hash, 'hex');
   179    const got = Buffer.from(actual, 'hex');
   180    return expected.length === got.length && 
crypto.timingSafeEqual(expected, got);
   181  }

   183  function nextSequence(tx, name) {
   184    const row = tx.prepare('SELECT value FROM sequences WHERE name = 
?').get(name);
   185    if (!row) throw new Error(`Missing sequence ${name}`);
   186    const next = Number(row.value) + 1;
   187    tx.prepare('UPDATE sequences SET value = ? WHERE name = ?').run(next, 
name);
   188    return next;
   189  }

   221  function getAuthToken(req) {
   222    const auth = req.get('Authorization') || '';
   223    const match = auth.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i);
   224    return match ? match[1].trim() : null;
   225  }
   226  
   227  function resolveTokenUser(token) {
   228    if (!token) return null;
   229    const tokenHash = hashToken(token);
   230    const row = db.prepare(`
   231      SELECT t.user_id, u.name, u.email, u.role
   232      FROM auth_tokens t
   233      JOIN users u ON u.id = t.user_id
   234      WHERE t.token_hash = ? AND t.revoked_at IS NULL
   235    `).get(tokenHash);
   236    return row ? { id: row.user_id, name: row.name, email: row.email, 
role: row.role } : null;
   237  }
   238  
   239  function requireAuth(req) {
   240    const token = getAuthToken(req);
   241    const user = resolveTokenUser(token);
   242    if (!user) return null;
   243    return { user, token };
   244  }

   250  function courseForUser(user) {
   251    const course = getCourse();
   252    if (!course) return null;
   253    if (user.role === 'instructor' && course.instructor_id === user.id) 
return course;
   254    const enrollment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM enrollments WHERE 
course_id = ? AND user_id = ?').get(course.id, user.id);
   255    if (!enrollment && user.role !== 'instructor') return null;
   256    return course;
   257  }

   324  function computeExpiryIso(attempt, assessment, accommodation) {
   325    const started = Date.parse(attempt.started_at);
   326    const durationExpiry = new Date(started + (assessment.duration_minutes
+ (accommodation.extra_time_minutes || 0)) * 60000).toISOString();
   327    const dueExpiry = new Date(Date.parse(assessment.due_at) + 
(accommodation.deadline_extension_minutes || 0) * 60000).toISOString();
   328    return new Date(Math.min(Date.parse(durationExpiry), 
Date.parse(dueExpiry))).toISOString();
   329  }
   330  
   331  function attemptExpiry(attempt, assessment, accommodation) {
   332    return computeExpiryIso(attempt, assessment, accommodation);
   333  }
   334  
   335  function attemptIsExpired(attempt, assessment, accommodation) {
   336    return FIXED_NOW_MS >= Date.parse(attemptExpiry(attempt, assessment, 
accommodation));
   337  }
   338  
   339  function computeObjectiveScore(assessmentId, answersByItem) {
   340    const items = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM items WHERE assessment_id = ? 
AND kind = ? ORDER BY id').all(assessmentId, 'multiple_choice');
   341    let total = 0;
   342    for (const item of items) {
   343      const answer = answersByItem.get(item.id);
   344      if (typeof answer === 'string' && answer.trim() === 
String(item.answer).trim()) total += Number(item.points);
   345    }
   346    return total;
   347  }
   348  
   349  function criterionCompleteMap(attemptId) {
   350    const attempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attemptId);
   351    if (!attempt) return { complete: false, total: 0, count: 0, expected: 
0 };
   352    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = 
?').get(attempt.assessment_id);
   353    const criteria = db.prepare(`
   354      SELECT c.*
   355      FROM rubric_criteria c
   356      JOIN items i ON i.id = c.item_id
   357      WHERE i.assessment_id = ?
   358      ORDER BY c.id
   359    `).all(attempt.assessment_id);
   360    const grades = rubricGradesForAttempt(attemptId);
   361    const gradeMap = new Map(grades.map(g => [g.criterion_id, g]));
   362    let complete = true;
   363    let total = 0;
   364    for (const criterion of criteria) {
   365      const grade = gradeMap.get(criterion.id);
   366      if (!grade) {
   367        complete = false;
   368        continue;
   369      }
   370      total += Number(grade.score);
   371    }
   372    return { complete, total, count: grades.length, expected: 
criteria.length };
   373  }
   374  
   375  function recalcAttemptScores(tx, attemptId) {
   376    const attempt = tx.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attemptId);
   377    const assessment = tx.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = 
?').get(attempt.assessment_id);
   378    const answers = tx.prepare('SELECT item_id, value FROM answers WHERE 
attempt_id = ?').all(attemptId);
   379    const answersByItem = new Map(answers.map(a => [a.item_id, a.value]));
   380    const objective = computeObjectiveScore(attempt.assessment_id, 
answersByItem);
   381    const criteria = tx.prepare(`
   382      SELECT c.*
   383      FROM rubric_criteria c
   384      JOIN items i ON i.id = c.item_id
   385      WHERE i.assessment_id = ?
   386      ORDER BY c.id
   387    `).all(attempt.assessment_id);
   388    const grades = tx.prepare('SELECT * FROM rubric_grades WHERE 
attempt_id = ?').all(attemptId);
   389    const gradeMap = new Map(grades.map(g => [g.criterion_id, g]));
   390    let complete = true;
   391    let rubricTotal = 0;
   392    for (const criterion of criteria) {
   393      const grade = gradeMap.get(criterion.id);
   394      if (!grade) {
   395        complete = false;
   396        continue;
   397      }
   398      rubricTotal += Number(grade.score);
   399    }
   400    const status = criteria.length === 0 ? 'graded' : complete ? 'graded' 
: 'submitted';
   401    const total = complete ? objective + rubricTotal : null;
   402    tx.prepare(`
   403      UPDATE attempts
   404      SET objective_score = ?, rubric_score = ?, total_score = ?, status =
?, submitted_at = COALESCE(submitted_at, ?)
   405      WHERE id = ?
   406    `).run(objective, complete ? rubricTotal : null, total, status, 
attempt.submitted_at || referenceMoment, attemptId);
   407    return { objective, rubricTotal: complete ? rubricTotal : null, total,
status, complete };
   408  }

   410  function ensureGradeableAttempt(user, attempt) {
   411    if (!attempt) return notFound('Attempt not found.');
   412    if (attempt.feedback_status === 'released') return conflict('Released 
attempts are immutable.');
   413    if (user.role === 'instructor') return null;
   414    if (user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && attempt.assigned_grader_id 
=== user.id) return null;
   415    return forbidden('You may only grade assigned submissions.');
   416  }
   417  
   418  function ensureCourseAccess(user, course) {
   419    if (!course) return notFound('Course not found.');
   420    if (user.role === 'instructor' && course.instructor_id === user.id) 
return null;
   421    const enrollment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM enrollments WHERE 
course_id = ? AND user_id = ?').get(course.id, user.id);
   422    if (!enrollment) return forbidden('You are not enrolled in this 
course.');
   423    return null;
   424  }

   430    `).run(course_id, actor_user_id, actor_role, event_type, target_type,

   438  function buildAssessmentPayload(assessment, user) {
   439    const items = assessmentItems(assessment.id).map(item => {
   440      const base = {
   441        id: item.id,
   442        kind: item.kind,
   443        prompt: item.prompt,
   444        points: item.points,
   445      };
   446      if (item.kind === 'multiple_choice') {
   447        base.options = item.options;
   448        if (user.role !== 'student') base.answer = item.answer;
   449      } else {
   450        const criteria = itemCriteria(item.id).map(c => ({ id: c.id, 
label: c.label, maxPoints: c.max_points }));
   451        if (user.role !== 'student') base.criteria = criteria;
   452      }
   453      return base;
   454    });
   455    const policyRow = db.prepare('SELECT weight_pct FROM outcome_policy 
WHERE course_id = ? AND assessment_id = ?').get(assessment.course_id, 
assessment.id);
   456    return {
   457      id: assessment.id,
   458      courseId: assessment.course_id,
   459      title: assessment.title,
   460      status: assessment.status,
   461      opensAt: assessment.opens_at,
   462      dueAt: assessment.due_at,
   463      durationMinutes: assessment.duration_minutes,
   464      maxAttempts: assessment.max_attempts,
   465      publishedAt: assessment.published_at,
   466      weightPct: policyRow ? Number(policyRow.weight_pct) : 
(assessment.status === 'published' ? 0 : null),
   467      itemCount: items.length,
   468      items,
   469    };
   470  }

   472  function buildAttemptPayload(attempt, user, course) {
   473    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id =

   484      startedAt: attempt.started_at,

   488      feedbackStatus: attempt.feedback_status,
   489      objectiveScore: attempt.feedback_status === 'released' || user.role

   490      rubricScore: attempt.feedback_status === 'released' || user.role !==

   491      totalScore: attempt.feedback_status === 'released' || user.role !==

   495    const savedAnswers = answersForAttempt(attempt.id).reduce((acc, row)

   536  function buildOutcomeLedger(user, course, assessments, attempts, 
policyRows, exceptionRows) {
   537    const students = db.prepare(`
   538      SELECT u.id, u.name, u.email
   539      FROM enrollments e
   540      JOIN users u ON u.id = e.user_id
   541      WHERE e.course_id = ? AND e.kind = 'student'
   542      ORDER BY u.name
   543    `).all(course.id);
   544    const publishedAssessments = assessments.filter(a => a.status === 
'published');
   545    const weightByAssessment = new Map(policyRows.map(r => 
[r.assessment_id, Number(r.weight_pct)]));
   546    const excMap = new Map(exceptionRows.map(r => 
[`${r.student_id}:${r.assessment_id}`, r]));
   547    const rows = [];
   548    for (const student of students) {
   549      const row = {
   550        studentId: student.id,
   551        studentName: student.name,
   552        studentEmail: student.email,
   553        assessments: [],
   554        availableTotal: 0,
   555        finalPercentage: null,
   556        finalStatus: 'pending',
   557        explanation: '',
   558      };
   559      let includedWeight = 0;
   560      let weightedSum = 0;
   561      let pending = false;
   562      for (const assessment of publishedAssessments) {
   563        const weight = weightByAssessment.get(assessment.id) ?? 0;
   564        const exception = excMap.get(`${student.id}:${assessment.id}`) || 
null;
   565        const attempt = latestAttemptForAssessmentStudent(assessment.id, 
student.id);
   566        const attemptPayload = attempt ? buildAttemptPayload(attempt, 
user.role === 'student' ? { role: 'student' } : user, course) : null;
   567        const released = attempt && attempt.feedback_status === 
'released';
   568        const cell = {
   569          assessmentId: assessment.id,
   570          assessmentTitle: assessment.title,
   571          weightPct: weight,
   572          state: 'missing',
   573          score: null,
   574          maxPoints: null,
   575          released: released,
   576          excused: exception ? Boolean(exception.excused) : false,
   577        };
   578        if (weight <= 0) {
   579          cell.state = 'unweighted';
   580        } else if (exception && exception.excused) {
   581          cell.state = 'excused';
   582        } else if (!attempt) {
   583          cell.state = 'missing';
   584          pending = true;
   585        } else if (!released) {
   586          cell.state = attempt.status === 'in_progress' ? 'pending' : 
'pending';
   587          pending = true;
   588        } else {
   589          const maxPoints = db.prepare('SELECT COALESCE(SUM(points), 0) AS
total FROM items WHERE assessment_id = ?').get(assessment.id).total;
   590          const totalScore = attempt.total_score ?? 
(attempt.objective_score || 0) + (attempt.rubric_score || 0);
   591          cell.state = 'released';
   592          cell.score = totalScore;
   593          cell.maxPoints = maxPoints;
   594          includedWeight += weight;
   595          const percentage = maxPoints > 0 ? (totalScore / maxPoints) : 0;
   596          weightedSum += weight * percentage;
   597        }
   598        if (weight > 0 && !(exception && exception.excused)) {
   599          if (!attempt || !released) {
   600            pending = true;
   601          }
   602        }
   603        row.assessments.push(cell);
   604      }
   605      row.availableTotal = includedWeight;
   606      if (!pending && includedWeight > 0) {
   607        row.finalPercentage = Number((100 * weightedSum / 
includedWeight).toFixed(2));
   608        row.finalStatus = 'calculated';
   609        row.explanation = 'All included published work has released 
scores.';
   610      } else if (includedWeight === 0) {
   611        row.finalStatus = 'unavailable';
   612        row.explanation = 'No weighted published assessments are currently
included.';
   613      } else {
   614        row.finalStatus = 'pending';
   615        row.explanation = 'One or more included published assessments are 
missing or unreleased.';
   616      }
   617      rows.push(row);
   618    }
   619    if (user.role === 'student') {
   620      return rows.filter(row => row.studentId === user.id);
   621    }
   622    return rows;
   623  }

   625  function filterAuditForUser(user, course, events) {
   626    if (user.role === 'instructor') return events;
   627    const attemptsById = new Map(attemptsByCourse(course.id).map(a => 
[a.id, a]));
   628    return events.filter(event => {
   629      if (event.actor_user_id === user.id) return true;
   630      if (event.target_type !== 'attempt') return false;
   631      const attempt = attemptsById.get(event.target_id);
   632      if (!attempt) return false;
   633      if (user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && attempt.assigned_grader_id
=== user.id) return true;
   634      if (user.role === 'student' && attempt.student_id === user.id) 
return true;
   635      return false;
   636    });
   637  }

   639  function buildWorkspace(user) {
   640    const course = courseForUser(user);
   641    if (!course) return null;
   642    const assessments = assessmentRows(course.id).map(a => 
buildAssessmentPayload(a, user));
   643    const attempts = attemptsByCourse(course.id)
   644      .filter(attempt => {
   645        if (user.role === 'instructor') return true;
   646        if (user.role === 'teaching_assistant') return 
attempt.assigned_grader_id === user.id;
   647        return attempt.student_id === user.id;
   648      })
   649      .map(attempt => buildAttemptPayload(attempt, user, course));
   650    const policyRows = outcomePolicy(course.id);
   651    const exceptionRows = outcomeExceptionRows(course.id);
   652    const ledger = user.role === 'teaching_assistant' ? [] : 
buildOutcomeLedger(user, course, assessments.map(a => ({ id: a.id, title: 
a.title, status: a.status })), attemptsByCourse(course.id), policyRows, 
exceptionRows);
   653    const audit = filterAuditForUser(user, course, 
auditRows(course.id)).map(event => ({
   654      id: event.id,
   655      eventType: event.event_type,
   656      targetType: event.target_type,
   657      targetId: event.target_id,
   658      summary: event.summary,
   659      actorUserId: event.actor_user_id,
   660      actorRole: event.actor_role,
   661      createdAt: event.created_at,
   662    }));
   663    const queue = user.role === 'teaching_assistant'
   664      ? attemptsByCourse(course.id)
   665          .filter(attempt => attempt.assigned_grader_id === user.id && 
attempt.feedback_status !== 'released' && attempt.status !== 'in_progress')
   666          .map(attempt => buildAttemptPayload(attempt, user, course))
   667      : [];
   668    const releasedPreviews = user.role === 'instructor'
   669      ? db.prepare('SELECT * FROM release_previews WHERE course_id = ?

   673        attempts: JSON.parse(row.selection_json),
   674        consumedAt: row.consumed_at,
   675        createdAt: row.created_at,
   676      }))
   677      : [];
   678    return {
   679      user: userSummary(user),
   680      course: {
   681        id: course.id,
   682        title: course.title,
   683        instructorId: course.instructor_id,
   684        revision: currentRevision(),
   685        referenceMoment,
   686      },
   687      assessments,
   688      attempts,
   689      gradebook: user.role === 'teaching_assistant' ? queue : ledger,
   690      outcomePolicy: user.role === 'teaching_assistant' ? [] : 
policyRows.map(r => ({ assessmentId: r.assessment_id, weightPct: 
Number(r.weight_pct) })),
   691      outcomeExceptions: user.role === 'teaching_assistant' ? [] : 
exceptionRows.map(r => ({
   692        studentId: r.student_id,
   693        assessmentId: r.assessment_id,
   694        excused: Boolean(r.excused),
   695        reason: r.reason,
   696      })),
   697      audit,
   698      releasePreviews: releasedPreviews,
   699      permissions: {
   700        canAuthorAssessments: user.role === 'instructor',
   701        canGrade: user.role === 'instructor' || user.role === 
'teaching_assistant',
   702        canRelease: user.role === 'instructor',
   703        canEditOutcomes: user.role === 'instructor',
   704      },
   705    };
   706  }

   746  function validateAssessmentCreate(body) {
   747    if (!isPlainObject(body)) return badRequest('bad_body', 'Request body 
must be a JSON object.');
   748    const title = normalizeText(body.title);
   749    const opensAt = parseDateIso(body.opensAt);
   750    const dueAt = parseDateIso(body.dueAt);
   751    const durationMinutes = parsePositiveInteger(body.durationMinutes);
   752    const maxAttempts = parsePositiveInteger(body.maxAttempts);
   753    if (!title) return unprocessable('Assessment title is required.');
   754    if (!opensAt || !dueAt) return unprocessable('Opening and due times 
must be parseable dates in UTC.');
   755    if (Date.parse(opensAt) >= Date.parse(dueAt)) return 
unprocessable('Opening time must be before due time.');
   756    if (!durationMinutes) return unprocessable('Duration must be a 
positive whole minute value.');
   757    if (!maxAttempts) return unprocessable('Attempt limit must be a 
positive whole number.');
   758    return { title, opensAt, dueAt, durationMinutes, maxAttempts };
   759  }
   760  
   761  function validateQuestionCreate(body) {
   762    if (!isPlainObject(body)) return badRequest('bad_body', 'Request body 
must be a JSON object.');
   763    const kind = normalizeText(body.kind);
   764    const prompt = normalizeText(body.prompt);
   765    const points = parseFiniteNumber(body.points);
   766    if (!['multiple_choice', 'written'].includes(kind)) return 
unprocessable('Question kind must be multiple_choice or written.');
   767    if (!prompt) return unprocessable('Question prompt is required.');
   768    if (!(points > 0)) return unprocessable('Question points must be 
positive.');
   769    if (kind === 'multiple_choice') {
   770      const options = Array.isArray(body.options) ? 
body.options.map(option => normalizeText(option)).filter(Boolean) : null;
   771      const answer = normalizeText(body.answer);
   772      if (!options || options.length < 2) return 
unprocessable('Multiple-choice questions need at least two distinct options.');
   773      const unique = new Set(options.map(opt => opt.trim()));
   774      if (unique.size !== options.length) return 
unprocessable('Multiple-choice options must be unique after trimming.');
   775      if (!answer || !unique.has(answer)) return 
unprocessable('Multiple-choice key must match one option.');
   776      return { kind, prompt, points, options, answer };
   777    }
   778    const rubricCriteria = Array.isArray(body.rubricCriteria) ? 
body.rubricCriteria : [];
   779    if (!rubricCriteria.length) return unprocessable('Written questions 
need at least one positive rubric criterion.');
   780    const criteria = [];
   781    for (const criterion of rubricCriteria) {
   782      if (!isPlainObject(criterion)) return unprocessable('Rubric criteria
must be objects.');
   783      const label = normalizeText(criterion.label);
   784      const maxPoints = parseFiniteNumber(criterion.maxPoints);
   785      if (!label) return unprocessable('Rubric criteria require labels.');
   786      if (!(maxPoints > 0)) return unprocessable('Rubric criteria require 
positive maximum points.');
   787      criteria.push({ label, maxPoints });
   788    }
   789    return { kind, prompt, points, criteria };
   790  }
   791  
   792  function validatePolicyBody(body, publishedAssessments) {
   793    if (!isPlainObject(body)) return badRequest('bad_body', 'Request body 
must be a JSON object.');
   794    const rows = Array.isArray(body.weights) ? body.weights : null;
   795    if (!rows) return unprocessable('Policy weights must be provided as an
array.');
   796    if (rows.length !== publishedAssessments.length) return 
unprocessable('Policy must include every published assessment exactly once.');
   797    const seen = new Set();
   798    let total = 0;
   799    const parsed = [];
   800    for (const row of rows) {
   801      if (!isPlainObject(row)) return unprocessable('Policy rows must be 
objects.');
   802      const assessmentId = normalizeText(row.assessmentId);
   803      const weightPct = parseFiniteNumber(row.weightPct);
   804      if (!assessmentId) return unprocessable('Policy rows require 
assessment IDs.');
   805      if (seen.has(assessmentId)) return unprocessable('Policy cannot 
include duplicate assessments.');
   806      seen.add(assessmentId);
   807      const assessment = publishedAssessments.find(a => a.id === 
assessmentId);
   808      if (!assessment) return unprocessable('Policy may include only 
published assessments in this course.');
   809      if (!(weightPct >= 0)) return unprocessable('Policy weights must be 
nonnegative percentages.');
   810      if (!/^\d+(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(String(row.weightPct).trim())) {
   811        const numericText = String(row.weightPct).trim();
   812        if (!/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(numericText)) return 
unprocessable('Policy weights must have at most two decimal places.');
   813        const decimals = numericText.split('.')[1] || '';
   814        if (decimals.length > 2) return unprocessable('Policy weights must
have at most two decimal places.');
   815      }
   816      total += weightPct;
   817      parsed.push({ assessmentId, weightPct });
   818    }
   819    if (Math.abs(total - 100) > 0.00001) return unprocessable('Policy 
weights must total exactly 100%.');
   820    return parsed;
   821  }

   823  function validateExceptionBody(body, course) {
   824    if (!isPlainObject(body)) return badRequest('bad_body', 'Request body 
must be a JSON object.');
   825    const studentId = normalizeText(body.studentId);
   826    const assessmentId = normalizeText(body.assessmentId);
   827    const excused = Boolean(body.excused);
   828    const reason = normalizeText(body.reason);
   829    if (!studentId || !assessmentId) return unprocessable('Student and 
assessment IDs are required.');
   830    const assessment = findAssessmentById(assessmentId);
   831    if (!assessment || assessment.course_id !== course.id || 
assessment.status !== 'published') return unprocessable('Only published 
assessments in this course may be excused.');
   832    const enrollment = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM enrollments WHERE 
course_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND kind = 'student'").get(course.id, studentId);
   833    if (!enrollment) return unprocessable('Exception student must be 
enrolled in this course.');
   834    if (excused && !reason) return unprocessable('Excusing an assessment 
requires a reason.');
   835    return { studentId, assessmentId, excused, reason: excused ? reason : 
null };
   836  }

   916  function settleExpiredAttempts() {
   917    const course = getCourse();
   918    if (!course) return;
   919    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
   920      const inProgress = db.prepare(`
   921        SELECT a.*
   922        FROM attempts a
   923        JOIN assessments ass ON ass.id = a.assessment_id
   924        WHERE ass.course_id = ? AND a.status = 'in_progress'
   925        ORDER BY a.started_at ASC, a.id ASC
   926      `).all(course.id);
   927      let changed = false;

   929        const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id 
= ?').get(attempt.assessment_id);
   930        const accommodation = accommodationForStudent(course.id, 
attempt.student_id);

WHERE attempt_id = ?').all(attempt.id);
   933        const answersByItem = new Map(answers.map(a => [a.item_id, 
a.value]));
   934        const objective = computeObjectiveScore(attempt.assessment_id, 
answersByItem);

   936          SELECT c.*
   937          FROM rubric_criteria c
   938          JOIN items i ON i.id = c.item_id
   939          WHERE i.assessment_id = ?
   940        `).all(attempt.assessment_id);
   941        const finalStatus = criteria.length === 0 ? 'graded' : 
'submitted';

   943          UPDATE attempts
   944          SET status = ?, submitted_at = ?, objective_score = ?, 
rubric_score = ?, total_score = ?, feedback_status = 'hidden'
   945          WHERE id = ?
   946        `).run(finalStatus, referenceMoment, objective, criteria.length

   950          actor_role: 'system',
   951          event_type: 'attempt_auto_submitted',

   957      }
   958      if (changed) {
   959        bumpRevision(db);
   960      }

  1181  function recordToken(tx, userId) {
  1182    const token = randomToken();
  1183    tx.prepare('INSERT INTO auth_tokens (token_hash, user_id, issued_at) 
VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run(hashToken(token), userId, referenceMoment);
  1184    return token;
  1185  }
  1186  
  1187  function invalidateAllTokens(tx, userId) {
  1188    tx.prepare('UPDATE auth_tokens SET revoked_at = ? WHERE user_id = ? 
AND revoked_at IS NULL').run(referenceMoment, userId);
  1189  }

  1191  function signInHandler({ tx, body }) {
  1192    const email = normalizeText(body.email);
  1193    const password = typeof body.password === 'string' ? body.password : 
'';
  1194    if (!email || !password) return badRequest('missing_credentials', 
'Email and password are required.');
  1195    const user = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE lower(email) = 
lower(?)').get(email);
  1196    if (!user || !verifyPassword(password, user)) return 
unauthorized('Invalid email or password.');
  1197    const token = recordToken(tx, user.id);
  1198    return responseJson(200, {
  1199      ok: true,
  1200      token,

  1252  function publishAssessmentHandler({ tx, user, expectedRevision }, 
assessmentId) {
  1253    const course = courseForUser(user);
  1254    const access = ensureAuthorCanEditAssessment(user, course);
  1255    if (access) return access;
  1256    const assessment = findAssessmentById(assessmentId);
  1257    if (!assessment || assessment.course_id !== course.id) return

  1258    if (assessment.status === 'published') return conflict('Assessment was
already published.');
  1259    if (expectedRevision !== currentRevision()) {
  1260      return responseJson(409, { ok: false, error: { code: 
'stale_revision', message: 'Another tab updated the course. Refresh and try 
again.' }, revision: currentRevision(), workspace: buildWorkspace(user) });
  1261    }
  1262    const itemCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM items 
WHERE assessment_id = ?').get(assessmentId).count;
  1263    if (!itemCount) return unprocessable('Publishing requires at least one
question.');
  1264    if (!(assessment.duration_minutes > 0)) return 
unprocessable('Publishing requires a positive duration.');
  1265    tx.prepare('UPDATE assessments SET status = ?, published_at = ? WHERE 
id = ?').run('published', referenceMoment, assessmentId);
  1266    tx.prepare('INSERT INTO outcome_policy (course_id, assessment_id, 
weight_pct, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON

user.id, referenceMoment);
  1267    bumpRevision(tx);
  1268    createAudit(tx, { course_id: course.id, actor_user_id: user.id,

  1272  function startAttemptHandler({ tx, user, body, expectedRevision }, 
assessmentId) {
  1273    const course = courseForUser(user);
  1274    const access = ensureCourseAccess(user, course);
  1275    if (access) return access;
  1276    if (user.role !== 'student') return forbidden('Only students may start
attempts.');
  1277    const assessment = findAssessmentById(assessmentId);
  1278    if (!assessment || assessment.course_id !== course.id) return 
notFound('Assessment not found.');
  1279    if (assessment.status !== 'published') return conflict('Assessment is 
not published.');
  1280    const accommodation = accommodationForStudent(course.id, user.id);
  1281    const startedAllowed = Date.parse(referenceMoment) >= 
Date.parse(assessment.opens_at);
  1282    const effectiveDue = new Date(Date.parse(assessment.due_at) + 
(accommodation.deadline_extension_minutes || 0) * 60000).toISOString();
  1283    if (!startedAllowed) return conflict('Assessment has not opened 
yet.');
  1284    if (Date.parse(referenceMoment) > Date.parse(effectiveDue)) return 
conflict('The adjusted due time has passed.');
  1285    if (expectedRevision !== currentRevision()) {
  1286      return responseJson(409, { ok: false, error: { code: 
'stale_revision', message: 'Another tab updated the course. Refresh and try 
again.' }, revision: currentRevision(), workspace: buildWorkspace(user) });
  1287    }
  1288    const used = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM attempts WHERE 
assessment_id = ? AND student_id = ?').get(assessmentId, user.id).count;
  1289    const active = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE assessment_id 
= ? AND student_id = ? AND status = ?').get(assessmentId, user.id, 
'in_progress');
  1290    if (active) {
  1291      return responseJson(200, { ok: true, revision: currentRevision(), 
attemptId: active.id, workspace: buildWorkspace(user) });
  1292    }
  1293    if (used >= assessment.max_attempts) return conflict('No attempts 
remain for this assessment.');
  1294    const attemptId = nextReadableId(tx, 'AT', 'attempt');
  1295    tx.prepare('INSERT INTO attempts (id, assessment_id, student_id, 
status, started_at, submitted_at, assigned_grader_id, feedback_status, 
submission_kind, objective_score, rubric_score, total_score, released_at, 
auto_submitted_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 
?)').run(attemptId, assessmentId, user.id, 'in_progress', referenceMoment, null,
db.prepare('SELECT assigned_grader_id FROM attempts WHERE assessment_id = ? AND 
student_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1').get(assessmentId, 
user.id)?.assigned_grader_id || null, 'hidden', null, null, null, null, null, 
null);
  1296    bumpRevision(tx);
  1297    createAudit(tx, { course_id: course.id, actor_user_id: user.id, 
actor_role: user.role, event_type: 'attempt_started', target_type: 'attempt', 
target_id: attemptId, summary: `Started ${attemptId}` });
  1298    return responseJson(201, { ok: true, revision: currentRevision(), 
attemptId, workspace: buildWorkspace(user) });
  1299  }

  1301  function saveAnswerHandler({ tx, user, body, expectedRevision }, 
attemptId, itemId) {
  1302    const attempt = findAttemptById(attemptId);
  1303    if (!attempt) return notFound('Attempt not found.');
  1304    if (attempt.student_id !== user.id) return forbidden('You may only 
save your own attempt.');
  1305    if (attempt.status !== 'in_progress') return conflict('Only 
in-progress attempts accept answer saves.');
  1306    const assessment = findAssessmentById(attempt.assessment_id);
  1307    const course = courseForUser(user);
  1308    const access = ensureCourseAccess(user, course);
  1309    if (access) return access;
  1310    const accommodation = accommodationForStudent(course.id, user.id);
  1311    if (attemptIsExpired(attempt, assessment, accommodation)) return 
conflict('The attempt has expired and no longer accepts answers.');
  1312    if (expectedRevision !== currentRevision()) {
  1313      return responseJson(409, { ok: false, error: { code: 
'stale_revision', message: 'Another tab updated the course. Refresh and try 
again.' }, revision: currentRevision(), workspace: buildWorkspace(user) });
  1314    }
  1315    const item = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM items WHERE id = ? AND 
assessment_id = ?').get(itemId, attempt.assessment_id);
  1316    if (!item) return notFound('Question not found.');
  1317    const value = typeof body.value === 'string' ? body.value : '';
  1318    if (item.kind === 'multiple_choice') {
  1319      if (!value || !JSON.parse(item.options_json).includes(value)) return
unprocessable('Answer must match one of the available options.');
  1320    }
  1321    tx.prepare('INSERT INTO answers (attempt_id, item_id, value, 
updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(attempt_id, item_id) DO UPDATE SET 
value = excluded.value, updated_at = excluded.updated_at').run(attemptId, 
itemId, value, referenceMoment);
  1322    bumpRevision(tx);
  1323    createAudit(tx, { course_id: course.id, actor_user_id: user.id, 
actor_role: user.role, event_type: 'answer_saved', target_type: 'attempt', 
target_id: attemptId, summary: `Saved answer for ${attemptId}` });
  1324    return responseJson(200, { ok: true, revision: currentRevision(), 
attemptId, itemId, workspace: buildWorkspace(user) });
  1325  }
  1326  
  1327  function submitAttemptHandler({ tx, user, expectedRevision }, attemptId,
auto = false) {
  1328    const attempt = findAttemptById(attemptId);
  1329    if (!attempt) return notFound('Attempt not found.');
  1330    if (attempt.student_id !== user.id && user.role !== 'instructor') 
return forbidden('You may only submit your own attempt.');
  1331    if (attempt.status !== 'in_progress') {
  1332      if (attempt.status === 'submitted' || attempt.status === 'graded') {
  1333        return responseJson(200, { ok: true, revision: currentRevision(), 
attemptId, submitted: true, replay: true, workspace: buildWorkspace(user) });
  1334      }
  1335      return conflict('This attempt cannot be submitted again.');
  1336    }
  1337    const assessment = findAssessmentById(attempt.assessment_id);
  1338    const course = courseForUser(user);
  1339    if (user.role !== 'instructor') {
  1340      const access = ensureCourseAccess(user, course);
  1341      if (access) return access;
  1342      const accommodation = accommodationForStudent(course.id, user.id);
  1343      if (!auto && attemptIsExpired(attempt, assessment, accommodation)) 
return conflict('The attempt has expired and auto-submitted already.');
  1344    }
  1345    if (expectedRevision !== currentRevision()) {

  1506  function getProtectedWorkspace(req, res) {
  1507    const auth = requireAuth(req);
  1508    if (!auth) return res.status(401).json({ ok: false, error: { code: 
'unauthorized', message: 'Sign-in required.' } });
  1509    const workspace = buildWorkspace(auth.user);
  1510    if (!workspace) return res.status(403).json({ ok: false, error: { 
code: 'forbidden', message: 'You are not enrolled in this course.' } });
  1511    return res.json({ ok: true, ...workspace });
  1512  }

    function handleWrite(req, res, { requireUser = true, signIn = false, handler

  new_str: 
    function handleWrite(req, res, { requireUser = true, signIn = false, handler
}) {
      const body = validateRequestShape(req.body);
      if (!body) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: { code: 
'bad_body', message: 'Request body must be a JSON object.' } });
      const identity = validateOperationIdentity(req, body);
      if (!identity) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: { code: 
'bad_operation_id', message: 'X-Operation-Id must be a nonempty random string.' 
} });
      if (!signIn && identity.expectedRevision === null) {
        return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad_revision', 
message: 'X-Coursemark-Revision must be a nonnegative whole number.' } });
      }
      const auth = signIn ? null : requireAuth(req);
      if (requireUser && !auth) return res.status(401).json({ ok: false, error: 
{ code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Sign-in required.' } });
      const user = auth ? auth.user : null;
      const opMethod = req.method;
      const opPath = req.path;
      const run = db.transaction(() => {
        const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operations WHERE op_id = 
?').get(identity.opId);
        if (existing) {
          if (existing.method !== opMethod || existing.path !== opPath || 
existing.body_hash !== identity.bodyHash || (existing.user_id || null) !== (user
? user.id : null)) {
            return conflict('Operation identifier was reused for a different 
request.', { code: 'operation_mismatch' });
          }
          return { status: existing.status, body: 
JSON.parse(existing.response_json), replay: true };
        }
        const result = handler({
          tx: db,
          user,
          body,
          expectedRevision: identity.expectedRevision,
          opId: identity.opId,
        });
        if (!result || typeof result.status !== 'number' || !result.body) {
          throw new Error('Invalid handler response.');
        }
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO operations (op_id, user_id, method, path, body_hash, 
status, response_json, created_at)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(identity.opId, user ? user.id : null, opMethod, opPath, 
identity.bodyHash, result.status, JSON.stringify(result.body), referenceMoment);
        return result;
      });
      try {
        const result = run();
        res.status(result.status).json(result.body);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('write handler failed', error);
        res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: { code: 'server_error', 
message: 'An unexpected server error occurred.' } });
      }
    }

Summary: Add default grader lookup helper

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "insert"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  new_str: 
    
    function defaultGraderId(courseId) {
      const row = db.prepare(`
        SELECT u.id
        FROM enrollments e
        JOIN users u ON u.id = e.user_id
        WHERE e.course_id = ? AND u.role = 'teaching_assistant'
        ORDER BY u.name
        LIMIT 1
      `).get(courseId);
      return row ? row.id : null;
    }

I’m writing the interactive browser application now.

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"

  file_text: 
    const app = document.getElementById('app');
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal');
    
    const state = {
      token: localStorage.getItem('coursemark_token') || '',
      user: null,
      workspace: null,
      view: 'courses',
      message: 'Ready.',
      messageKind: 'info',
      lastTrigger: null,
      modal: null,
      refreshing: false,
    };
    
    function escapeHtml(value) {
      return String(value ?? '')
        .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
        .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
        .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
        .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
        .replaceAll("'", '&#39;');
    }
    
    function safeText(value) {
      return escapeHtml(value ?? '');
    }
    
    function fmtDate(iso) {
      if (!iso) return '—';
      const date = new Date(iso);
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone: 'UTC',
        dateStyle: 'medium',
        timeStyle: 'short',
      }).format(date) + ' UTC';
    }
    
    function fmtNumber(value, digits = 2) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return '—';
      const num = Number(value);
      if (!Number.isFinite(num)) return '—';
      return num.toLocaleString('en-GB', { minimumFractionDigits: digits, 
maximumFractionDigits: digits });
    }
    
    function viewLabel(view) {
      return ({
        courses: 'Courses',
        assessments: 'Assessments',
        attempts: 'Attempts',
        gradebook: 'Gradebook',
        audit: 'Audit',
      })[view] || view;
    }
    
    function badge(label, cls) {
      return `<span class="badge ${cls}">${safeText(label)}</span>`;
    }
    
    function currentRevision() {
      return state.workspace?.course?.revision ?? 0;
    }
    
    function setMessage(message, kind = 'info') {
      state.message = message;
      state.messageKind = kind;
      const live = document.getElementById('status-live');
      if (live) live.textContent = message;
    }
    
    function showLogin(message = 'Sign in to continue.') {
      state.user = null;
      state.workspace = null;
      state.view = 'courses';
      state.message = message;
      state.messageKind = 'info';
      render();
    }
    
    function saveToken(token) {
      state.token = token;
      localStorage.setItem('coursemark_token', token);
    }
    
    function clearToken() {
      state.token = '';
      localStorage.removeItem('coursemark_token');
    }
    
    function operationId() {
      return crypto.randomUUID();
    }
    
    async function request(path, options = {}) {
      const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});
      if (state.token && options.auth !== false) {
        headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${state.token}`);
      }
      if (options.body !== undefined) {
        headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
      }
      if (options.write) {
        headers.set('X-Operation-Id', options.operationId || operationId());
        if (!options.signIn) headers.set('X-Coursemark-Revision', 
String(currentRevision()));
      } else if (options.signIn) {
        headers.set('X-Operation-Id', options.operationId || operationId());
      }
      const response = await fetch(path, {
        method: options.method || 'GET',
        headers,
        body: options.body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : 
undefined,
      });
      const text = await response.text();
      let data = null;
      if (text) {
        try {
          data = JSON.parse(text);
        } catch {
          data = { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad_json', message: text } };
        }
      }
      if (!response.ok) {
        const error = new Error(data?.error?.message || response.statusText || 
'Request failed.');
        error.status = response.status;
        error.data = data;
        throw error;
      }
      return data;
    }
    
    function actionBusy(button, fn) {
      const target = button instanceof HTMLElement ? button : null;
      if (target) target.disabled = true;
      return Promise.resolve()
        .then(fn)
        .finally(() => {
          if (target) target.disabled = false;
        });
    }
    
    function openModal(html) {
      state.lastTrigger = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? 
document.activeElement : null;
      modal.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-inner"><div 
class="modal-head">${html.head}<button type="button" class="ghost" 
data-action="close-modal">Close</button></div><div 
class="modal-content">${html.body}</div></div>`;
      modal.showModal();
      const focusTarget = modal.querySelector('[data-focus="true"]') || 
modal.querySelector('button, input, select, textarea');
      if (focusTarget) focusTarget.focus();
    }
    
    function closeModal() {
      if (modal.open) modal.close();
      modal.innerHTML = '';
      const trigger = state.lastTrigger;
      state.lastTrigger = null;
      if (trigger && typeof trigger.focus === 'function') trigger.focus();
      state.modal = null;
    }
    
    function render() {
      if (!state.token || !state.user) {
        app.innerHTML = renderLogin();
        setMessage(state.message || 'Sign in to continue.');
        return;
      }
    
      const ws = state.workspace;
      const nav = ['courses', 'assessments', 'attempts', 'gradebook', 'audit'];
      app.innerHTML = `
        <div class="shell">
          <header class="topbar">
            <div class="topbar-inner">
              <div class="branding">
                <div>
                  <h1>Coursemark</h1>
                  <div class="helper">BIO-214 · Ecology and Field Methods</div>
                </div>
                <div class="row">
                  <button type="button" class="ghost" 
data-action="refresh">Refresh</button>
                  <button type="button" class="danger" 
data-action="sign-out">Sign out</button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="meta-row">
                <span class="pill"><strong>${safeText(state.user.name)}</strong>
· ${safeText(state.user.email)} · ${safeText(state.user.role.replaceAll('_', ' 
'))}</span>
                <span class="pill">Reference time: 
${safeText(fmtDate(ws?.course?.referenceMoment || 
'2026-09-02T12:00:00Z'))}</span>
                <span class="pill">Revision: 
${safeText(String(ws?.course?.revision ?? 0))}</span>
              </div>
              <nav class="nav" aria-label="Workspace navigation">
                ${nav.map(view => `<button type="button" data-view="${view}" 
${state.view === view ? 'aria-current="page"' : 
''}>${viewLabel(view)}</button>`).join('')}
              </nav>
              <div class="statusbar">
                <strong>Current identity:</strong>
                <span>${safeText(state.user.name)} 
(${safeText(state.user.role.replaceAll('_', ' '))})</span>
                <span class="status-live" id="status-live" role="status" 
aria-live="polite">${safeText(state.message)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </header>
          <main id="main" class="main" tabindex="-1">
            <section class="view ${state.view === 'courses' ? '' : 'hidden'}" 
data-view-panel="courses">${renderCoursesView(ws)}</section>
            <section class="view ${state.view === 'assessments' ? '' : 
'hidden'}" data-view-panel="assessments">${renderAssessmentsView(ws)}</section>
            <section class="view ${state.view === 'attempts' ? '' : 'hidden'}" 
data-view-panel="attempts">${renderAttemptsView(ws)}</section>
            <section class="view ${state.view === 'gradebook' ? '' : 'hidden'}" 
data-view-panel="gradebook">${renderGradebookView(ws)}</section>
            <section class="view ${state.view === 'audit' ? '' : 'hidden'}" 
data-view-panel="audit">${renderAuditView(ws)}</section>
          </main>
        </div>
      `;
      if (state.modal) renderModal();
      syncLiveRegion();
    }
    
    function renderLogin() {
      return `
        <main id="main" class="main" tabindex="-1">
          <section class="card grid two">
            <div class="stack">
              <div>
                <h1 class="heading">Coursemark sign in</h1>

            </aside>
          </section>
          <div class="status-live" role="status" 
aria-live="polite">${safeText(state.message)}</div>
        </main>
      `;
    }
    
    function renderCoursesView(ws) {
      if (!ws) return emptyState('No workspace available.');
      const course = ws.course;
      return `
        <section class="section-card stack">
          <div class="space-between">
            <div>
              <h2 class="heading">Course</h2>
              <p class="subheading">The local workspace for BIO-214 keeps 
assessments, attempts, grading and release work together.</p>
            </div>
            ${badge(course.revision >= 0 ? 'Live' : 'Offline', 'ok')}
          </div>
          <div class="grid two">
            <div class="card stack">
              <h3 class="heading">${safeText(course.title)}</h3>
              <div class="metrics">
                <span><strong>Course ID:</strong> ${safeText(course.id)}</span>
                <span><strong>Instructor:</strong> ${safeText(ws.user.role === 
'instructor' ? ws.user.name : 'Ada Mensah')}</span>
                <span><strong>Reference moment:</strong> 
${safeText(fmtDate(course.referenceMoment))}</span>
                <span><strong>Revision:</strong> 
${safeText(String(course.revision))}</span>
              </div>
              <p class="helper">Authentication is real and token-backed. A later
sign-in restores your server-side work, while sign-out revokes every session for
the account.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="card stack">
              <h3 class="heading">Identity and sync</h3>
              <div class="compact">
                <div><strong>Name:</strong> ${safeText(ws.user.name)}</div>
                <div><strong>Email:</strong> ${safeText(ws.user.email)}</div>
                <div><strong>Role:</strong> 
${safeText(ws.user.role.replaceAll('_', ' '))}</div>
                <div><strong>Sync:</strong> state lives in SQLite and all 
accepted writes are revisioned.</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      `;
    }
    
    function renderAssessmentsView(ws) {
      if (!ws) return emptyState('No assessments available.');
      const canAuthor = ws.permissions.canAuthorAssessments;
      return `
        <section class="section-card stack">
          <div class="space-between">
            <div>
              <h2 class="heading">Assessments</h2>
              <p class="subheading">Staff can inspect drafts and author new

            </div>
            <div class="row">
              <span class="pill">${badge(canAuthor ? 'Instructor authoring' : 
'Read-only', canAuthor ? 'ok' : 'unweighted')}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          ${canAuthor ? renderAssessmentCreateForm() : ''}
          <div class="cards ${ws.assessments.length > 1 ? 'two' : ''}">
            ${ws.assessments.map(assessment => renderAssessmentCard(assessment, 
ws.user, ws.permissions)).join('') || emptyCard('No assessments yet.')}
          </div>
        </section>
      `;
    }
    
    function renderAssessmentCreateForm() {
      return `
        <div class="card stack">
          <h3 class="heading">Create draft assessment</h3>
          <p class="helper">Opening and due times are interpreted as UTC.

          <form data-form="create-assessment" id="create-assessment-form">
            <div class="grid two">
              <label>Title <input name="title" required placeholder="e.g. Stream

            </div>
            <div class="grid two">
